# Skills · V · part 44

841 entries, voxel-debugging to vrs-test-quick. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **voxel-debugging**: Instrument-first diagnostic protocol for complex engine bugs — check known-bugs docs, locate code via CodeGraph, inspect live editor state, ship a diagnostic patch, and wait for logs before fixing. Use when the user asks you to fix a complex bug, undefined behavior, lighting issues, fluid flow problems, or chunk generation glitches. Once the root cause is understood and the system has a validation suite, hand off to the validation-driven-bugfix skill. ([A-Van-Gestel/Unity-Minecraft_Clone](https://github.com/A-Van-Gestel/Unity-Minecraft_Clone/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/voxel-debugging))
- **voxel-engine**: Hands-on tutor for building your own Voxel engine from scratch, milestone by milestone with runnable checkpoints. Use it whenever the user wants to build my own voxel engine, minecraft-like block world from scratch, chunk meshing, OpenGL voxel rendering, or says 自己写体素引擎、做 Minecraft 类方块世界、OpenGL 体素渲染、区块和网格生成, or picks the voxel-engine topic from build-your-own-x. It coaches and scaffolds (concept → skeleton → learner writes code → test) instead of handing over a finished solution, and links the curated upstream tutorials. ([linkxzhou/build-your-own-x-skills](https://github.com/linkxzhou/build-your-own-x-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/voxel-engine) · ★21)
- **voxel-engine-from-scratch**: Use when building a voxel (cube-based) world engine from first principles — chunking, mesh generation, terrain generation. Not for general 3D rendering or using an existing voxel engine. Triggers on: 'build a voxel engine', 'minecraft-style world generation', 'chunk meshing', 'greedy meshing algorithm', 'voxel terrain generation', 'implement a block world'. Covers chunk representation, face culling, greedy meshing, and noise-based terrain. ([Horonomos-laps/Yana-AI](https://github.com/Horonomos-laps/Yana-AI/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/voxel-engine-from-scratch) · ★13 · 4 copies)
- **voxel-engine-voxel**: Trigger when the user wants to learn, implement, debug, or extend "让我们做一个体素引擎" or a 体素引擎（Voxel Engine） build-your-own project using C++. Use this skill for staged Claude Code guidance, milestone planning, minimal implementation, and troubleshooting. - **voxel-layer**: VoxelLayer extends ObstacleLayer with a 3D voxel grid for proper volumetric raycasting. Instead of projecting all points into 2D and raytracing in the plane, it maintains a 3D column of voxels at each (x, y) cell and raytraces in 3D space. ([three1324/yeonjinautomotive](https://github.com/three1324/yeonjinautomotive/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/voxel-layer))

- **voxtral-transcription**: Voice-to-text transcription using Mistral AI's Voxtral Transcribe 2. Open-source (Apache 2.0), runs locally on phones and laptops with latency under 200ms for real-time translation. Supports 13 languages.
